# Third-party notices

Rynhult is licensed under the MIT License (see `LICENSE`). It is built on the open-source software below; each keeps its own license. Generated by `scripts/third_party_notices.py`; don't edit by hand.

Agent CLIs (Claude Code, Codex, GitHub Copilot CLI, Antigravity, Cursor, OpenCode) and services such as Caddy, tmux and git are installed separately under their own terms; Rynhult doesn't include them.

Web builds apply a local composition-boundary fix to @xterm/xterm 6.0.0 (MIT); see `web/scripts/xterm-composition.mjs`. Its original license notice is retained below.
